Legal cannabis stores in Canada

These are the stores each provincial regulator lists as licensed or authorized. A store on these lists can sell legally; a shop that is not on its province's list is not a legal store. Lists change often, so check the regulator's own list before you rely on one entry. For provinces not listed here yet, the province table on the Canada page says how legal sales work there.

OntarioBritish ColumbiaAlbertaSaskatchewanManitoba

Stores by province

Ontario: 1,916 stores authorized to open

Stores the AGCO lists as "Authorized to Open". Its file also lists applications in progress and cancelled stores; those are left out here.

Source: [1]
